## 值提取至點 (Spatial Analyst 工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Spatial Analyst 工具箱
- 提取分析工具集

**english**　Extract Values to Points

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/extract-values-to-points.html

**id**　d9e109790c98f8b987-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/extract-values-to-points.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Extract Values to Points，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入點圖徵 [in_point_features]、輸入網格 [in_raster]、輸出點圖徵 [out_point_features]、在點位置處進行插值 （可選） [interpolate_values]、將所有輸入網格屬性追加到輸出點圖徵 （可選） [add_attributes]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/spatial-analyst/extract-values-to-points.html#parameters

It can be an integer or floating-point type raster."}, {"parameter": "out_point_features", "label_zh": "輸出點圖徵", "label_en": "Output point features", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Feature Class", "description_en": "The output point feature dataset containing the extracted raster values."}, {"parameter": "interpolate_values", "label_zh": "在點位置處進行插值 （可選）", "label_en": "Interpolate values at the point locations (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether interpolation will be used. Checked—The value of the cell will be calculated from the adjacent cells with valid values using bilinear interpolation. NoData values will be ignored in the interpolation unless all adjacent cells are NoData. Unchecked—No interpolation will be applied; the value of the cell center will be used. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "add_attributes", "label_zh": "將所有輸入網格屬性追加到輸出點圖徵 （可選）", "label_en": "Append all the input raster attributes to the output point features (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Determines if the raster attributes are written to the output point feature dataset. Checked—All the fields from the input raster (except Count) will be added to the point attributes. Unchecked—Only the value of the input raster is added to the point attributes. This is the default."}]

## How Generate Threshold Drive Times works · How Generate Threshold Drive Times works

**id**　d9eea7178ac8d73341-1

**english**　How Generate Threshold Drive Times works

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Business Analyst 工具箱
- 貿易區工具集
- 貿易區域工具集概念

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/business-analyst/how-generate-threshold-drive-times-works.html

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/business-analyst/how-generate-threshold-drive-times-works.html

**text**　需要具有 Business Analyst 許可才能使用。
The Generate Threshold Drive Times tool allows you to generate drive-time trade areas that use the street network and expand outward until the threshold criterion is reached. The size of the output polygon is determined by the value provided in the Threshold Values parameter for the selected threshold variable from the Business Analyst dataset.
Generate Threshold Drive Times is dependent on the input point feature class, the threshold variable, and the threshold values. Because the geoprocessing tool has distance-based parameters, it uses the network dataset to perform the solve.

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/business-analyst/how-generate-threshold-drive-times-works.html

## How Generate Threshold Drive Times works · Algorithm details

**id**　d9eea7178ac8d73341-2

**english**　How Generate Threshold Drive Times works

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Business Analyst 工具箱
- 貿易區工具集
- 貿易區域工具集概念

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/business-analyst/how-generate-threshold-drive-times-works.html#374

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/business-analyst/how-generate-threshold-drive-times-works.html

**text**　The concept of a root-finding algorithm is used in the Generate Threshold Drive Times tool. While the goal of a root-finding algorithm is to find the zeros for function F(t), the algorithm implemented in this tool is optimized, and the goal is to reach close to the minimum of function F(t) instead of exactly zero.
THvalue—Threshold value (constant), provided by the user.
THvariable—Threshold variable used in the analysis, for example, 2026 Total Population. The DTpoly will be enriched with this variable at each iteration.
DTpoly(Point(xy),t)—Drive-time polygon around point with coordinates (xy) and distance unit value (t).
The algorithm is iterative, and the end goal is to find distance unit (t), where F(t) is equal to almost zero (or zero). At each iteration, the algorithm computes a new estimate of (t), calculates the new value of F(t), and checks the different stop conditions. The iteration stops when any of the stop conditions (listed below) is met. The value of distance unit (t) is then used to generate a drive-time polygon surrounding the traversable streets. This polygon is then apportioned and enriched with the threshold variable, and the enriched value is closer to the threshold value.

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/business-analyst/how-generate-threshold-drive-times-works.html#374

## How Generate Threshold Drive Times works · Algorithm details

**id**　d9eea7178ac8d73341-3

**english**　How Generate Threshold Drive Times works

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Business Analyst 工具箱
- 貿易區工具集
- 貿易區域工具集概念

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/business-analyst/how-generate-threshold-drive-times-works.html#374

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/business-analyst/how-generate-threshold-drive-times-works.html

**text**　The following parameters in the geoprocessing tool are stop conditions for the root-finding algorithm:
Iterations Limit—The number of iterations to find the distance unit value (t) where F(t) is zero. This is an optional parameter.
Minimum Step—The minimum distance units between one threshold area candidate and the next as the model approaches the threshold value to prevent infinite iterations. This is an optional parameter.
Threshold Percent Difference—The maximum percentage difference between the target value and threshold value that will be used when determining the threshold drive time, for example, 5 percent. The default value is 5.

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/business-analyst/how-generate-threshold-drive-times-works.html#374

## How Generate Threshold Drive Times works · 注:

**id**　d9eea7178ac8d73341-4

**english**　How Generate Threshold Drive Times works

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Business Analyst 工具箱
- 貿易區工具集
- 貿易區域工具集概念

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/business-analyst/how-generate-threshold-drive-times-works.html#374

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/business-analyst/how-generate-threshold-drive-times-works.html

**text**　As the algorithm is optimized to reach the minimum of function F(t), the root-solving algorithm will not always reach zero. This is because the iterations can continue indefinitely in trying to reach exactly zero, which will impact the performance of the tool. To avoid this, the tool will iterate until the minimum step between two iterations is 2 seconds or 22 meters. The default minimum step will be overridden if a user inputs a different value.
The distance unit value (t) from the root-finding algorithm is used to generate the drive-time polygons, which are based on the Service Area algorithm. The goal of the Service Area algorithm is to return a polygon containing the subset of connected edge features such that they are within the specified network distance or cost cutoff.

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/business-analyst/how-generate-threshold-drive-times-works.html#374

## How Generate Threshold Drive Times works · Iterate time values, drive-time trade areas, and apportionment

**id**　d9eea7178ac8d73341-7

**english**　How Generate Threshold Drive Times works

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Business Analyst 工具箱
- 貿易區工具集
- 貿易區域工具集概念

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/business-analyst/how-generate-threshold-drive-times-works.html#4D3

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/business-analyst/how-generate-threshold-drive-times-works.html

**text**　The tool then adopts the root-finding algorithm and iterates multiple times until function F(t) equals zero. For each value of time (t) that the root solving algorithm iterates through, the solver creates a drive-time trade area using time value (t). It then enriches each trade area with the threshold variable (for this example, 2026 Total Households). This iteration continues until F(t) equals zero (or almost zero). At this point, the solver now has a time value (t) that will be used in the next step.

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/business-analyst/how-generate-threshold-drive-times-works.html#4D3

**text**　Once the time value (t) is found where function F(t) is equal to zero (or almost zero), a final drive-time trade area is created around the input proposed community centers.
The following image shows the drive-time polygons that are generated around the two input community centers:
You can see that the two polygons are not the same size. The reason for this is that Uptown Charlotte (lower left polygon) has a greater population density, which means higher total households in smaller areas, as compared to the Charlotte suburbs. In terms of the algorithm, this means that the root-solving algorithm found different values of (t) for the two input community centers, where F(t) equals zero.

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/110001-120000/tool-errors-and-warnings-110351-110375-110371.html#description

**text**　顯示的圖徵至少具有一項無法計算的局部統計資料。 由于以下各種原因，可能無法計算圖徵的局部統計資料：
該圖徵不具有相鄰圖徵。
所有相鄰圖徵的權重均為零或為空。
偏度和分位數不平衡要求相鄰圖徵值中至少有兩個唯一值。
如果相鄰圖徵值的四分位距為零，則可能無法計算分位數不平衡。

## Prepare Aviation Data (Aviation Tools) · 用法

**id**　d9f23d1e6dd6da13c3-2

**english**　Prepare Aviation Data

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Aviation 工具箱
- 圖表工具集
- 資料管理工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/aviation/prepare-aviation-data.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/aviation/prepare-aviation-data.html

**text**　Attributes will be migrated to the cartographic features in the format || <> <value> <> || in which features are delimited by || and the attribute values of those features are delimited by <>. For example, to migrate the TYPE_CODE, IDENT_TXT, and MAGNETICVARIATION_VAL features from the main ADHP feature class, the output in the ADHP_C cartographic feature class will be <> TYPE_CODE <> IDENT_TXT <> MAGNETICVARIATION_VAL <>. 注: The || symbols may not be included in the migrated information if a single feature is being migrated from the main features to the cartographic features. 提示： The ArcGIS Aviation Charting product data files contain Arcade scripts for parsing this delimited data. These scripts are located at <installation location>\ArcGIS Aviation Charting\Product Files\<version>\SampleConfigs\ICAO\Enroute\ArcadeExpressions.
The optional Input Datasets parameter is populated by a list of tables and feature classes from the provided JSON. You can select specific tables and features classes for evaluation. If none are selected, they will all be evaluated. 注意： There are existing limitations with using a file geodatabase that will affect the performance of this tool.

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/aviation/prepare-aviation-data.html#usage

## Prepare Aviation Data (Aviation Tools) · 用法

**id**　d9f23d1e6dd6da13c3-3

**english**　Prepare Aviation Data

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Aviation 工具箱
- 圖表工具集
- 資料管理工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/aviation/prepare-aviation-data.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/aviation/prepare-aviation-data.html

**text**　This tool will run on all the charts in a project unless individual charts or chart series are specified with the Area of Interest Features parameter. Select the charts to process in the area of interest (AOI) layer and use them as the Area of Interest Features parameter value making sure to turn on the Use the selected records toggle button below the parameter.
The Configuration File (.json) parameter lists all of the rules used to extract information from the main aviation data and migrates the information to the cartographic features so that it can be used for symbology and labeling expressions. For each cartographic feature class, a list of rules can be added, with each consisting of the following elements: calcfield—The required field on the cartographic feature where the output will be written. This is required. fields—A list of the main feature fields from which information will be extracted. Fields from multiple related tables can be used. This is required. tables—A list of the tables or feature classes from which attribute information will be extracted. All of the tables referenced by a field in the fields list should be included. This is required. where—An SQL expression that defines the relationships between the tables and, optionally, filters the results. This is required. distinct—Specifies whether duplicate values will be removed from the results and only distinct values will be written to the calcfield field (true). This is optional. requires—A property that sets dependencies for the calcfield elements to determine the order in which they are written. This is optional.

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/aviation/prepare-aviation-data.html#usage

## Prepare Aviation Data (Aviation Tools) · 用法

**id**　d9f23d1e6dd6da13c3-4

**english**　Prepare Aviation Data

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Aviation 工具箱
- 圖表工具集
- 資料管理工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/aviation/prepare-aviation-data.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/aviation/prepare-aviation-data.html

**text**　There is no set order for the calcfield elements when they are processed from the .json configuration file. There may be times when a calcfield value has a dependency on the output of another calcfield value such as the following: When a calcfield value depends on the output of another calcfield value, you must add a requires key value pair to the calcfield value in the JSON. The value is a string with a key value pair of the table and the calcfield value must be processed before the current calcfield value. For example, "requires":"airspace_c.service_info". Alternatively, you can also provide a list if the output of multiple other calcfield elements are required, for example, "requires":["airspace_c.service_info", "navaidsystem_c.fss_info"]. When listing a required calcfield value that also has its own dependency, it is not necessary to also list its required field. However, it must have its own requires key value pair in the JSON.

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/aviation/prepare-aviation-data.html#usage

**id**　d9f23d1e6dd6da13c3-5

**english**　Prepare Aviation Data

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Aviation 工具箱
- 圖表工具集
- 資料管理工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/aviation/prepare-aviation-data.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/aviation/prepare-aviation-data.html

**text**　This tool will adjust the processing order to ensure that any required dependencies are run first.
The following sample configuration file can be used to populate the Configuration File (.json) parameter: { "adhp_c":[{ "calcfield":"adhp_info", "fields": [ "adhp.TYPE_CODE", "adhp.IDENT_TXT", "adhp.MAGNETICVARIATION_VAL" ], "tables": [ "adhp" ], "where": "Master_ID = adhp.gfid" }, { "calcfield":"airspace_info", "fields": [ "airspace.class_code", "airspace.workhr_code" ], "tables": [ "adhp", "airspace" ], "where": "Master_ID = adhp.gfid and adhp.gfid = airspace.adhp_id and airspace.class_code IN ('C', 'D')" }], "navaidsystem": [ { "calcfield":"availability_info", "fields": [ "availability.operationstatus_code" ], "tables": [ "navaidsystem", "availability" ], "where": "navaidsystem.gfid = availability.predicated_id" }, { "calcfield":"navaidcomponent_info", "fields": [ "navaidcomponent.subtype_code", "navaidcomponent.slavedvariation_val", "navaidcomponent.channel_txt", "navaidcomponent.frequency_val", "navaidcomponent.status_code", "navaidcomponent.workhr_code", "navaidcomponent.voice_code" ], "tables": [ "navaidsystem", "navaidassociation", "navaidcomponent" ], "where": "navaidsystem.gfid = navaidassociation.navaidsystem_id and navaidassociation.navaidcomponent_id = navaidcomponent.gfid", "distinct" : true, "requires": ["adhp_c.adhp_info", "adhp_c.availability_info"] } ] The migration data will be stored in the target feature class in the calcfield value. This is populated with the values, such as attributes, from the fields value, which target the source feature class. 注: The distinct property allows duplicate values to be removed from the results.

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/aviation/prepare-aviation-data.html#usage

## Prepare Aviation Data (Aviation Tools) · 代碼示例

**id**　d9f23d1e6dd6da13c3-19

**english**　Prepare Aviation Data

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Aviation 工具箱
- 圖表工具集
- 資料管理工具集

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/aviation/prepare-aviation-data.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/aviation/prepare-aviation-data.html

**text**　import arcpy

# Input database where data
target_gdb = r"C:\data\MyData.gdb"

# config file for data calculation
config_file = r"C:\data\config.json"

# subset of tables or feature classes that will be calculated
in_datasets = "adhp;navaidsystem"

#Input your area of interest features
aoi_features = r"C:\data\MyData.gdb\AOI"

# Running tool
arcpy.aviation.PrepareAviationData(target_gdb, config_file, in_datasets, aoi_features)

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/aviation/prepare-aviation-data.html#derived-output

## Prepare Aviation Data (Aviation Tools) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Aviation 工具箱
- 圖表工具集
- 資料管理工具集

**english**　Prepare Aviation Data

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/aviation/prepare-aviation-data.html

**id**　d9f23d1e6dd6da13c3-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/aviation/prepare-aviation-data.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Prepare Aviation Data，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。Target Geodatabase [target_gdb]、Configuration File (.json) [config_file]、Input Datasets （可選） [in_dataset_names]、Area of Interest Features （可選） [aoi_features]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/aviation/prepare-aviation-data.html#parameters

## 002625：注記圖徵類將就地修改。 運行此工具之前備份資料。 · 描述

**id**　d9fca7dffef966b4c6-1

**english**　002625: The annotation feature class will be modified in place. Back up data before running this tool.

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 工具錯誤和警告
- 2601-2700

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-02601-02625-002625.html#description

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-02601-02625-002625.html

**text**　注記圖徵類將就地修改。
更改圖徵類的參考比例是一個資源消耗很大的操作，需要更新所有圖徵的邊界框幾何。 參考比例更改將導致圖徵大小更改，從而可能導致注記或尺寸注記圖徵重疊。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-02601-02625-002625.html#description

## 拓撲工具集概述 · 拓撲工具集概述

**id**　da115228e23ed5e85c-1

**english**　An overview of the Topology toolset

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 資料管理工具箱
- 拓撲工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/an-overview-of-the-topology-toolset.html

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/an-overview-of-the-topology-toolset.html

**text**　“拓撲”工具集包含一組可用于創建和管理地理資料庫拓撲的工具。
地理資料庫拓撲幫助確保資料完整性。 拓撲的使用提供了一種對資料執行完整性檢查的機制，有助于在地理資料庫中驗證和保持更好的圖徵表示。 此外，還可以使用拓撲為圖徵之間的多種空間關系建模。 這為多種分析操作（如查找相鄰圖徵、處理圖徵之間的重疊邊界以及沿連接圖徵進行導航）提供了支持。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/data-management/an-overview-of-the-topology-toolset.html

**id**　da115228e23ed5e85c-5

**english**　An overview of the Topology toolset

**chapter**　
- 工具參考

**parameter**　

**interface**　

**headers**　
- 工具
- 描述

**values**　
- 導出拓撲錯誤
- 將錯誤和異常從地理資料庫拓撲導出到目標地理資料庫。 將導出與錯誤和異常相關聯的所有信息，如錯誤或異常所引用的圖徵。 導出錯誤和異常后，可使用任何許可級別的 ArcGIS 訪問圖徵類。 圖徵類可與按位置選擇圖層工具配合使用，并可共享給無權訪問拓撲的其他用戶。

## 重新構建地址定位器 (地理編碼工具) · 匯總

**id**　da11a9ec0337c2f74e-1

**english**　Rebuild Address Locator

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 地理編碼工具箱

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/geocoding/rebuild-address-locator.html#summary

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/geocoding/rebuild-address-locator.html

**text**　重新構建地址定位器以使用當前參考資料更新定位器。 由于定位器在創建時便包含參考資料的快照，因此在更改參考資料的幾何和屬性時，地址定位器并不會使用更新后的資料對地址進行地理編碼。 要使用最新的參考資料對地址進行地理編碼，必須重新構建定位器，才能更新定位器中的更改內容。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/geocoding/rebuild-address-locator.html#summary

## 重新構建地址定位器 (地理編碼工具) · 用法

**id**　da11a9ec0337c2f74e-2

**english**　Rebuild Address Locator

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 地理編碼工具箱

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/geocoding/rebuild-address-locator.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/geocoding/rebuild-address-locator.html

**text**　您必須具有地址定位器的寫入權限才能使用此工具。
要重新構建定位器，必須具有所有引用的圖徵類和表。 如果自從上次創建定位器后將參考資料移動到了其他位置，則重新構建定位器將失敗。 或者，可以使用創建定位器工具來重新創建定位器。
基于版本化地理資料庫的定位器將使用創建定位器時所用的相同版本來重新構建。
不能使用此工具重新構建復合定位器。 管理復合定位器的最佳做法是在地理處理模型中使用創建復合地址定位器 工具創建復合定位器。 在 ModelBuilder 中，還可以將此工具與創建定位器工具結合起來使用，以將其輸出用作復合定位器的輸入，并管理復合定位器的整個創建或更新過程。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/geocoding/rebuild-address-locator.html#usage

## 重新構建地址定位器 (地理編碼工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 地理編碼工具箱

**english**　Rebuild Address Locator

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/geocoding/rebuild-address-locator.html

**id**　da11a9ec0337c2f74e-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/geocoding/rebuild-address-locator.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Rebuild Address Locator，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入地址定位器 [in_address_locator]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/geocoding/rebuild-address-locator.html#parameters

- 為用戶授予或撤消 （可選）
- 指定是將角色添加到用戶或用戶列表，還是從角色中移除用戶或用戶列表。 授予角色—該角色將授予指定的一個或多個用戶，使其成為角色的成員。 這是默認設置。 撤消角色—該角色將從指定的一個或多個用戶中撤消，以將其從角色中移除。
- String

- 指定是將角色添加到用戶或用戶列表，還是從角色中移除用戶或用戶列表。 GRANT—該角色將授予指定的一個或多個用戶，使其成為角色的成員。 這是默認設置。 REVOKE—該角色將從指定的一個或多個用戶中撤消，以將其從角色中移除。
- String

## 創建角色 (資料管理工具) · 代碼示例

**id**　da1b4dbb40a4e1eccb-24

**english**　Create Role

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 資料管理工具箱
- 地理資料庫管理工具集

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/create-role.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/create-role.html

**text**　"""
Name: create_database_role.py
Description: Provide connection information to a database user.
Type create_database_role.py -h or create_database_role.py --help for usage
"""

# Import system modules
import arcpy
import os
import optparse
import sys

# Define usage and version
parser = optparse.OptionParser(usage = "usage: %prog [Options]", version="%prog 1.0 for 10.1 release")

#Define help and options
parser.add_option ("--DBMS", dest="Database_type", type="choice", choices=['SQLSERVER', 'ORACLE', 'POSTGRESQL', ''], default="", help="Type of enterprise DBMS:  SQLSERVER, ORACLE, or POSTGRESQL.")
parser.add_option ("-i", dest="Instance", type="string", default="", help="DBMS instance name")
parser.add_option ("-D", dest="Database", type="string", default="none", help="Database name:  Not required for Oracle")
parser.add_option ("--auth", dest="Account_authentication", type ="choice", choices=['DATABASE_AUTH', 'OPERATING_SYSTEM_AUTH'], default='DATABASE_AUTH', help="Authentication type options (case-sensitive):  DATABASE_AUTH, OPERATING_SYSTEM_AUTH.  Default=DATABASE_AUTH")
parser.add_option ("-U", dest="Dbms_admin", type="string", default="", help="DBMS administrator user")
parser.add_option ("-P", dest="Dbms_admin_pwd", type="string", default="", help="DBMS administrator password")
parser.add_option ("-o", dest="operation", type ="choice", choices=['GRANT', 'REVOKE'], default='GRANT', help="Specify which operation to perform: grant to or revoke from the user(s). Options (case-sensitive):  GRANT, REVOKE.  Default=GRANT")
parser.add_option ("-r", dest="role", type="string", default="", help="role to be granted to the user")
parser.add_option ("-u", dest="userlist", type="string", default="", help="List of users to grant to or revoke from, separated by comma.")

# Check if value entered for option
try:
    (options, args) = parser.parse_args()

    #Check if no system arguments (options) entered
    if len(sys.argv) == 1:
        print("%s: error: %s\n" % (sys.argv[0], "No command options given"))
        parser.print_help()
        sys.exit(3)

    #Usage parameters for spatial database connection
    database_type = options.Database_type.upper()
    instance = options.Instance
    database = options.Database.lower()
    account_authentication = options.Account_authentication.upper()
    dbms_admin = options.Dbms_admin
    dbms_admin_pwd = options.Dbms_admin_pwd
    userlist = options.userlist
    role = options.role
    operation = options.operation

    if (database_type == "SQLSERVER"):
        database_type = "SQL_SERVER"

    if( database_type ==""):
        print(" \n%s: error: \n%s\n" % (sys.argv[0], "DBMS type (--DBMS) must be specified."))
        parser.print_help()
        sys.exit(3)

    if (role == ""):
        print(" \n%s: error: \n%s\n" % (sys.argv[0], "Role name (-r) must be specified."))
        parser.print_help()
        sys.exit(3)

    if (database_type == "SQL_SERVER"):
        if (account_authentication == "DATABASE_AUTH" and dbms_admin == ""):
            print("\n%s: error: %s\n" % (sys.argv[0], "DBMS administrator must be specified with database authentication"))
            sys.exit(3)
        if (account_authentication == "OPERATING_SYSTEM_AUTH" and dbms_admin != ""):
            print("\nWarning: %s\n" % ("Ignoring DBMS administrator specified when using operating system authentication..."))
    else:
        if (dbms_admin == ""):
            print("\n%s: error: %s\n" % (sys.argv[0], "DBMS administrator must be specified!"))
            sys.exit(3)

    # Get the current product license
    product_license=arcpy.ProductInfo()

    # Checks required license level
    if product_license.upper() == "ARCVIEW" or product_license.upper() == 'ENGINE':
        print("\n" + product_license + " license found!" + " Creating a role in an enterprise geodatabase or database requires an ArcGIS for Desktop Standard or Advanced, ArcGIS Engine with the Geodatabase Update extension, or ArcGIS for Server license.")
        sys.exit("Re-authorize ArcGIS before creating enterprise geodatabase.")
    else:
        print("\n" + product_license + " license available!  Continuing to create...")
        arcpy.AddMessage("+++++++++")

    # Local variables
    instance_temp = instance.replace("\\","_")
    instance_temp = instance_temp.replace("/","_")
    instance_temp = instance_temp.replace(":","_")
    Conn_File_NameT = instance_temp + "_" + database + "_" + dbms_admin

    if os.environ.get("TEMP") == None:
        temp = "c:\\temp"
    else:
        temp = os.environ.get("TEMP")

    if os.environ.get("TMP") == None:
        temp = "/usr/tmp"
    else:
        temp = os.environ.get("TMP")

    Connection_File_Name = Conn_File_NameT + ".sde"
    Connection_File_Name_full_path = temp + os.sep + Conn_File_NameT + ".sde"

    # Check for the .sde file and delete it if present
    arcpy.env.overwriteOutput=True
    if os.path.exists(Connection_File_Name_full_path):
        os.remove(Connection_File_Name_full_path)

    try:
        print("\nCreating Database Connection File...\n")
        # Process: Create Database Connection File...
        # Usage:  out_file_location, out_file_name, DBMS_TYPE, instnace, account_authentication, username, password, database, save_username_password(must be true)
        arcpy.management.CreateDatabaseConnection(out_folder_path=temp, out_name=Connection_File_Name, database_platform=database_type, instance=instance, database=database, account_authentication=account_authentication, username=dbms_admin, password=dbms_admin_pwd, save_user_pass="TRUE")
        for i in range(arcpy.GetMessageCount()):
            if "000565" in arcpy.GetMessage(i):  # Check if database connection was successful
                arcpy.AddReturnMessage(i)
                arcpy.AddMessage("\n+++++++++")
                arcpy.AddMessage("Exiting!!")
                arcpy.AddMessage("+++++++++\n")
                sys.exit(3)
            else:
                arcpy.AddReturnMessage(i)
                arcpy.AddMessage("+++++++++\n")

        print("Creating database role...\n")
        arcpy.management.CreateRole(input_database=Connection_File_Name_full_path, grant_revoke=operation, role=role,  user_name=userlist)
        for i in range(arcpy.GetMessageCount()):
            arcpy.AddReturnMessage(i)
        arcpy.AddMessage("+++++++++\n")
    except:
        for i in range(arcpy.GetMessageCount()):
            arcpy.AddReturnMessage(i)

# Check if no value entered for option
except SystemExit as e:
    if e.code == 2:
        parser.usage = ""
        print("\n")
        parser.print_help()
        parser.exit(2)

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/data-management/create-role.html#derived-output

**text**　地理資料庫管理工具集概述
查找地理處理工具

## 創建角色 (資料管理工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 資料管理工具箱
- 地理資料庫管理工具集

**english**　Create Role

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/create-role.html

**id**　da1b4dbb40a4e1eccb-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/create-role.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Create Role，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入資料庫連接 [input_database]、角色 [role]、為用戶授予或撤消 （可選） [grant_revoke]、用戶名 （可選） [user_name]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/data-management/create-role.html#parameters

**english**　Create Role

**id**　da1b4dbb40a4e1eccb-schema

**kind**　版本參數表

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/create-role.html#parameters

**source_language**　en

**content_version**　3.8

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/data-management/create-role.html#parameters

**parameters**　[{"parameter": "input_database", "label_zh": "輸入資料庫連接", "label_en": "Input Database Connection", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Workspace", "description_en": "The connection file to a database or enterprise geodatabase. Connect as a database administrator user."}, {"parameter": "role", "label_zh": "角色", "label_en": "Role", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The name of the database role to create. If it's an existing role, type the name for the role you want to add users to or remove them from."}, {"parameter": "grant_revoke", "label_zh": "為用戶授予或撤消 （可選）", "label_en": "Grant To or Revoke From User(s) (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies whether the role will be added to a user or list of users or a user or list of users will be removed from the role. Grant role—The role will be granted to the specified user or users, making them a member of the role. This is the default. Revoke role—The role will be revoked from the specified user or users, removing them from the role."}, {"parameter": "user_name", "label_zh": "用戶名 （可選）", "label_en": "User Name(s) (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The name of the user whose role membership will change. To specify multiple users, type the user names separated by commas (no spaces)."}]

**signatures**　
- arcpy.management.CreateRole(input_database, role, {grant_revoke}, {user_name})

**text**　依英文版參數表順序列出：input_database、role、grant_revoke、user_name。選填標示、資料型別與英文原說明見 parameters。
